### [9] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现了AINTMA，一个多代理、自主化的测试管理架构，结合强化学习优先级、生成式质量说明和零信任云安全，在12个项目18个月评估中显著提升测试效率与缺陷捕获率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modern software quality assurance requires intelligent, autonomous systems that can make adaptive decisions across distributed cloud environments to reduce test cycle time, catch defects earlier, and scale to large test suites.

Method: 在云原生微服务上部署六类专用AI代理，RL优先级代理将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用47个特征和滚动36个月历史执行数据训练；生成式质量代理使用大型语言模型产出自然语言质量叙述和测试建议；安全通过零信任API网关(OAuth2/JWT)、加密代理间消息和多租户隔离实现。评估在12个异构项目、18个月期间进行，比较随机、商业基线并计算APFD、周期时间、缺陷逃逸率与ROI。

Result: AINTMA: a six-agent agentic AI architecture (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, RL Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, Cloud Security Monitor) on cloud-native microservices with zero-trust security. Reported outcomes: 88.4% APFD (vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline), 43% test cycle time reduction, defect escape rate drop from 8.3% to 2.1%, 340% ROI with 9-month payback, scales to 50k+ tests with <400ms response, generative intelligence rated 4.3/5 usefulness.

Conclusion: AINTMA表明将多代理协调、生成式智能和安全互联结合的agentic AI可以显著改进云规模企业的软件质量管理，但论文需补充更多可复现性细节、统计分析与安全评估细节以验证广泛适用性。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [10] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在医学领域部署LLM水印存在实质风险——只有通过人类专家驱动的、领域化的评估才能发现并量化这些风险，建议在部署前进行严格领域测试并寻求更保守或术语感知的水印策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印评估主要基于通用基准，但医疗文本对细微信息敏感，哪怕微小的token级扰动也可引发临床上重要的语义偏差，故需要探索水印在医学场景下的真实影响。

Method: 作者在11个LLM和7个VLM上对5种水印方案进行了系统基准测试，覆盖单模态与多模态临床推理任务；引入经人类医学专家验证的审计流程，对医学推理质量、术语精确性和诱发幻觉进行了分层评估，补充了传统自动化评估指标。

Result: 多数水印方案在医疗任务上引入了可观的性能下降和新型错误：词表破坏导致可读性/可诊断性降低，水印诱发不实术语或错误影像描述，聚合指标往往未能捕捉这些细粒度故障。

Conclusion: 水印会在医学领域对LLM的输出造成显著负面影响，导致词汇破坏、术语幻觉、影像所见误归因或遗漏等多种失败模式；在缺乏领域化评估和细粒度指标的情况下，汇总性基准可能掩盖这些临床相关错误，因此在医疗场景部署水印前必须进行领域特定验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [11] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个结合Transformer嵌入与语言学特征的点击诱饵检测系统，基于XGBoost达到91% F1，并封装为可即时提示用户的浏览器扩展，带可解释性与简短剧透功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测并提示用户互联网上的“clickbait”诱导性标题，从而帮助用户避免被误导阅读文章，提高上网信息质量和阅读效率。

Method: 比较了从传统向量化器到大型语言模型嵌入的多种NLP表示，构造语言学驱动特征和自定义“baitness”指标，将所有特征输入XGBoost分类器；在合并公开数据集上评估并部署到浏览器扩展，提供得分、可解释性指标与短摘要生成。

Result: 基于transformer嵌入与语言学特征的混合特征，训练出XGBoost模型，在开放合并数据集上达到F1=91%。实现了浏览器扩展：在打开前后提供点击诱饵概率（“baitness”得分）、解释性指标及一段1–2句的“clickbait spoiler”。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了混合特征加上树模型在点击诱饵检测任务上的高效性，并将检测结果以用户友好形式集成到浏览器扩展中。但若要确立可靠性与广泛适用性，还需补充数据、实验和可解释性细节。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [12] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 同一模型的随机重跑只提供每题不确定性（顶多一维信号），而由不同模型组成的多样化集合能揭示多个跨题信号维度，从而更好地暴露模型不知道的方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test whether intra-model stochastic variation (used in self-consistency/majority-voting uncertainty estimates) reveals cross-question structure (correlated flips) in the same way a diverse ensemble does.

Method: Compared two regimes on identical question sets: (A) single LLM run 100 times at temperature τ=1; (B) an ensemble of 24 distinct LLMs run once each at τ=0. Applied Marchenko–Pastur random-matrix analysis to answer covariance across questions to separate signal eigenvalues from sampling noise. Benchmarks: MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K across five question families. Also used a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null model with Monte Carlo draws for significance testing.

Result: Within single-model repeated runs, at most one eigenvalue exceeded the Marchenko–Pastur noise edge across datasets; across the 24-model ensemble, four eigenvalues cleared the noise edge. A matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produced at most one above-noise eigenvalue in 500 Monte Carlo draws, supporting that ensemble signals are nontrivial. Conclusion: self-consistency yields accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; ensemble diversity surfaces additional latent error structure.

Conclusion: Self-consistency (many stochastic runs of the same model) provides good per-question uncertainty but does not reveal cross-question correlated structure; a diverse ensemble of different LLMs does reveal multiple cross-question signal dimensions.

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [13] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JAXBench为TPU kernel优化提供了标准化基准与基线，显示文档条件化和搜索结构能显著提升生成内核的正确性与性能，但高质量专用算子优化仍具挑战性，并释放了数据与工具以促进社区贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏与GPU相当的、面向TPU的可优化核基准，阻碍AI驱动TPU kernel优化进展；需要统一目标与评测以驱动研究与工程改进。

Method: 设计并公开JAXBench：50个JAX工作负载（17个从生产MaxText提取，33个从KernelBench翻译并重设问题规模），8个带有Tokamax/Pallas手工优化核作为上界基线；用四种反馈驱动方法生成Pallas内核并评估（包含Autocomp beam-search和Gemini 3 Flash模型），测量正确率与速度提升。

Result: 使用经整理的TPU文档将每样本正确率从5.8%提高到37.3%，在50项基准中解决48项，带来1.28x geomean加速；Autocomp beam-search在全套达1.36x geomean；在8个手工调优核上Autocomp达1.60x（Tokamax上界2.08x），但在paged/ragged attention等专用算子仍落后。

Conclusion: JAXBench建立了面向TPU的基准套件，证明在Pallas等稀疏文档DSL上，针对性硬件上下文比模型规模更重要；通过文档条件化和搜索结构可显著提升生成的TPU kernel正确率和性能，但在复杂专用算子上仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [14] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap 是一个训练无关的并行解码框架，通过逐步验证因果依赖和草稿引导的前瞻机制，可靠地缓解 JPDE，在不中断生成质量的情况下为 dLLMs 带来显著推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行解码因 JPDE 需采用保守置信阈值，导致多余的去噪迭代与推理速度低下。目标是在不牺牲生成质量的前提下，放宽置信门限并在中等置信区间实现可靠加速。

Method: 提出训练无关的框架 DC-Leap，包含两大机制：1) Dynamic Contiguous Verification：在并行解码中逐步验证严格有序的因果依赖，以保证已生成 token 间的依赖关系不被破坏，从而避免 JPDE 引起的错误；2) Draft-guided decoding：先生成草稿并利用草稿做前瞻（跨多 token 跳跃）以扩展上下文，保持双向注意力的结构性优势，结合动态验证完成最终并行输出。

Result: 在多个标准基准上实验验证了 DC-Leap 的有效性：对长序列生成任务（MBPP）可实现最高 53.19x 的加速；当与 KV-Cache 结合时可达 105.02x。在这些加速下生成质量与基线可比。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DC-Leap 能在不重训练的前提下，通过引入动态连续验证（Dynamic Contiguous Verification）与草稿引导解码（draft-guided decoding）来中和联合概率依赖误差（JPDE），从而在“中等置信度”区间安全地并行加速 dLLMs，同时保持与基线相近的生成质量。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [15] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferenceBench 是一个开放式、时限驱动的推理性能优化基准，揭示了现有代理能改进已知工程流程但在提出并系统评估多样化策略方面不足，超参搜索在相同预算下仍更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准常限制动作空间或依赖既有“配方”，难以区分代理是真正优化能力还是记住了解决方案；因此需要一个更开放、真实的工程任务来测量代理在未完全可检索问题上的探索与优化能力。

Method: 构建开放式基准 InferenceBench：为代理分配一台 H100、目标 LLM、两小时时限与四种优化情景（prefill 延迟、decode 延迟、并发吞吐、综合平衡），任务是部署 OpenAI 兼容推理服务并最大化推理速度；评估代理行为与基线（PyTorch、vLLM、简单超参搜索）。

Result: 在 15 种前沿代理配置下，代理相较于朴素 PyTorch 基线可稳定提升，最高 8.08×；常与默认服务引擎持平或超越（vLLM 4.05×）；但仍不及相同预算下的简单超参数搜索（最高 11.53×）。轨迹分析显示代理会枚举多种技术但主要收敛到单一推理框架、只测试少数配置并花大量预算在重复测量与调参上，表明瓶颈是提出多样化配置与系统评估的能力，而非领域知识。

Conclusion: 该基准反映了代理在开放式 AI 工程环境中的局限：记忆到的解决方案能带来改进，但要实现更大收益需增强配置生成与系统化评估能力；未来可通过鼓励策略多样性、改进实验设计与噪声鲁棒的评估方法来提升代理表现。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [16] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DecodeShare 协议：在解码时(hidden states)识别跨任务一致的低维共享子空间，并在推理阶段移除该子空间以检验其因果作用；发现该子空间对决策性能有显著影响，且与 activation steering 存在重叠，投影可以分离功能并提高部署时 steering 的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型用同一组参数处理多任务，但在带 KV-cache 的推理阶段是否存在跨任务共用的解码结构尚不明确；理解该结构可帮助定位解码因果通道、改进激活引导（steering）策略并提升部署可靠性。

Method: 在多个任务和输入上收集 decode-time 隐藏态，使用降维/子空间识别方法（例如 SVD/主成分/子空间对齐等）提取低维共享子空间；在推理阶段，通过投影或移除该子空间（受限干预预算）来测量对下游决策性能和 logits/采样行为的影响；与在 prefill 时得到的子空间及随机基比较，并分析 steering 向量与该子空间的重叠性以及投影后行为。

Result: 实验表明：1) 发现的 decode-shared 子空间维度低但干预效果大，对决策性能的破坏显著优于对等预算的 prefill-derived 或随机子空间干预；2) steering 方向常重叠该子空间，直接在 decode-time 测评 steering 向量比用 prefill 代理更稳健；3) 将该子空间投影出可分离两类功能，为更可控的激活引导与诊断提供操作手段。

Conclusion: 解码期存在一个紧凑且具因果影响的任务通道（decode-shared 子空间）；干扰或移除该子空间在推理阶段会显著破坏决策，比对预填充(prefill)-派生或随机子空间的干预更具破坏性；该子空间与常见 steering 方向重叠，投影可以解耦两种功能，使得在 decode-time 评估 steering 更可靠。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [17] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PlanE——一个包含数据分解、指令微调与提示推理的抽取型 LLM 构建框架，并辅以 DTI 规划器自动选择 base-LLM 与组合，实验表明该方法在多数据集与多模型设置下均能提升效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对构建任务特定 LLM 需要大量指令微调数据导致的注释开销大与缺乏面向任务优化方法的问题，提出一种系统化、可选配的构建流程以降低标注成本并提高构建效率。

Method: 提出了 PlanE：包含（1）数据分解（将复杂任务分解为抽取子任务并生成训练样本）、（2）指令微调（基于分解后的数据对 base-LLM 进行微调/指令调优）、（3）提示推理（在推理阶段结合分解策略与模板）；并引入 DTI 规划器用于自动选择或搜索最佳 base-LLM 与 DTI 组合，兼顾性能与成本。

Result: 实验从两方面验证 PlanE：在不同数据集上使用相同 base-LLM，以及在相同数据集上使用不同 base-LLM；结果显示 PlanE 能提升性能且 DTI 规划器在不同优化目标下具有良好泛化性。代码已公开。

Conclusion: PlanE 框架能有效构建面向抽取任务的任务特定 LLM，通过数据分解、指令微调与提示推理三大环节提升模型在下游抽取任务上的能力；DTI 规划器可在给定数据集下选取最优的 base-LLM 与数据-微调-推理（DTI）组合，从而提高构建效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [18] [ExecuGraph: A Multi-Agent, Execution-Grounded Framework for Reliable Backend Code Synthesis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20499)
*Sai Deekshith Lekkala,Jothi Prabha Appadurai,Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Manpreet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExecuGraph通过多代理+执行驱动验证改进后端代码合成的可运行性，方法可配置为一镜像、重试或逐代理消融，实验显示对强基模型有较大收益但在小样本与部分任务上不总显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升后端代码合成的正确性与运行时可靠性，通过将执行验证置于多代理工作流的核心，从而克服单次生成不能保证正确性的缺陷。

Method: 在LangGraph上用本地模型（Ollama）实现六代理（Planner、Code Generator、Logical Reviewer、Evaluator、Optimizer、Explainer），可选检索层辅助算法召回；以子进程隔离的沙箱与实钟超时保证评估安全；通过有界重试预算与类型化有向流协调代理交互，并进行每代理消融与重试预算扫描以分析各要素贡献。

Result: 提出ExecuGraph，一个由六个专业代理组成、基于类型化有向工作流与有界重试预算的多代理框架；在internal-30、HumanEval与APPS子集上与一镜像与重试基线比较，呈现跨模型性能增益，尤其在较强基模型上多代理拆解的收益显著（例：DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite从57.5%提高到80.0%）。

Conclusion: 方法学贡献为统一代码库与可配置对照，支持精确量化每个设计要素的边际贡献；实验表明多代理框架在模型能力较高时收益更明显，但若干评估样本与统计证据仍需加强以确认普适性。

Abstract: Large Language Models generate plausible backend code, but a single-pass paradigm provides no guarantee of correctness or runtime reliability. We present ExecuGraph, a multi-agent framework that places execution-based validation at the center of backend code synthesis. Six specialized agents (Planner, Code Generator, Logical Reviewer, Evaluator, Optimizer, and Explainer) are coordinated by a typed directed workflow with a bounded retry budget, implemented on LangGraph with locally hosted models (Ollama) and an optional retrieval layer for algorithmic technique recall. A subprocess-isolated sandbox with a wall-clock timeout guards every evaluation. We evaluate on a curated 30-problem DSA suite (internal-30), HumanEval (n=64), and an APPS-introductory subset, contrasting ExecuGraph against a single-agent one-shot baseline and a single-agent execution-retry baseline (a Reflexion-style ablation that isolates the contribution of multi-agent decomposition). On internal-30, the three conditions are statistically indistinguishable (n=30; paired Wilcoxon p=0.59 MF vs. SO, p=0.08 SR vs. SO); 95% bootstrap confidence intervals on all pairwise mean differences include zero. On HumanEval, multi-full edges ahead by +3.1 pp. The strongest signal is cross-model: with DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite, graph-category accuracy improves from 57.5% (oneshot) to 80.0% (multi-full), a +22.5 pp jump that supports a scaling hypothesis: the value of multi-agent decomposition grows with base-model capability. The framework's primary contribution is methodological: a single codebase that collapses by configuration into one-shot, execution-retry, and per-agent ablation conditions, enabling controlled measurement of each lever's marginal contribution. A per-agent ablation, retry-budget sweep, error-class taxonomy, and test-source audit are reported.

</details>


### [19] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将贝叶斯劝导适配到生成式代理并在真实销售日志上建立可复现评测，发现当前模型的个性化诱导效果存在天花板，且在一些真实企业子集未能区分成功与失败。发布数据集与评测平台以促进进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化评测多为发件方适配（同一接收者为模型服务对象），缺乏检验为第三方（真实接收者）产生的消息是否能够实际诱导目标行为的可重复性基准与方法。通过采用有记录接触-行为对的销售场景，可在可复现的离线与小规模线上部署中评估生成式个性化的效果。

Method: 将Bayesian Persuasion框架适配到生成式代理，使用生成式模型为特定潜在客户生成个性化销售信息，并通过时序约束模拟与真实日志对齐的评估环境。

Result: 构建并公开SDR-Bench语料库（6,279条客户成功案例，覆盖22个行业、约200家企业），并发布SDR-Arena用于时序约束模拟评测。跨主流LLM与研究代理观察到个性化效果平台：模型在区分成功/不成功外联上未能显著优于基线（在一组Fortune 100技术企业上无统计学显著分离）；实地部署（12名资深商务代表）显示48%生成内容被评为立即有用，资深专家间评分一致性Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 目前生成式模型在典型商业外联场景的个性化说服能力有限，需更精细的接收方建模、随机化在线实验和因果推断工具来推动可再现的性能改进。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [20] [StrideDiffusion: Accelerating Diffusion Models for Time-series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20545)
*Du Yin,Estrid He,Julián Jerónimo Bañuelos,Yang Yang,Feng Hu,Yuchen Luo,Hao Xue,Stephan Sigg,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在反向扩散过程中按频带检测活动性来自适应调整步长，StrideDiffusion 在不重训练模型的前提下用远少的函数调用数实现时间序列扩散采样的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列扩散模型推理需大量顺序去噪步骤，通用的固定时间表浪费计算资源；而时间序列的不同频带在反向扩散中的演化速率不同，利用这种频谱差异可自适应缩短采样过程而不损质量。

Method: 在不改变模型权重或训练过程的条件下，算法在每次反向步骤评估若干频带指标：相对能量（band energy）、对数功率漂移（log-power drift）和相位速度（phase velocity）。基于这些度量判别高频是否仍在快速变化，若是则采取细粒度（小步长）去噪；否则以更大步长跳过若干时间步。作者在确定性仿射形式的反向更新下做了带级稳定性分析，证明非活跃频带在跳步情况下的变化关于跳长是线性的，从而为按频带活动调整步长提供局部正当性。

Result: StrideDiffusion 是一种无训练的频谱感知采样器，通过监测时序信号在反向扩散过程中不同频带的活动性（相对能量、对数功率漂移、相位速度），自适应选择逐步的步长：当高频带仍然活跃时使用小步长，当只剩低频结构时采用大跳步，从而显著减少推理时的去噪步骤数并保持或改善生成质量。作者还给出基于确定性仿射反向更新的逐频带稳定性分析，作为频谱活动性作为步长指示器的局部理论依据。实证上在六个无条件生成基准以及有条件插补和预测任务上均展示了 5–19x 的加速并保持可比的质量和预测性能，且代码公开。

Conclusion: 频域演化提供了一个实用且有理论局部支撑的信号，用以在时间序列扩散采样中动态选择步长；在多项基准上该方法可在保持或提升生成/预测质量的同时大幅减少推理开销。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become competitive generators for time series, but their practical use is limited by the large number of sequential denoising steps required at inference time. Existing fast samplers typically use fixed or generic timestep schedules, overlooking a distinctive property of time-series diffusion: different spectral bands evolve at different rates during the reverse process. We introduce StrideDiffusion, a training-free spectral-aware sampler that adaptively selects the denoising stride from band-level activity. At each step, StrideDiffusion monitors relative band energy, log-power drift, and phase velocity to identify whether high- frequency dynamics remain active or whether the trajectory is dominated by stable low-frequency structure. It then takes fine steps when rapidly varying bands are active and larger jumps once only coarse components remain. A bandwise stability analysis shows that inactive frequency bands change only linearly with the jump size under deterministic affine reverse updates, providing a local justification for spectral activity as a step-size indicator. Across six unconditional time-series generation benchmarks, StrideDiffusion uses only 14-66 function evaluations instead of 500/1000 denoising steps, achieving up to 18.9x wall-clock speedup while preserving or improving generation quality. On conditional imputation and forecasting, it further delivers 5-14x average acceleration with comparable predictive accuracy. These results show that spectral evolution provides a practical and principled signal for fast time-series diffusion sampling. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stridediff-ts.

</details>


### [21] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCGS通过在多轮对话中逐轮推断用户意图并用价值/遗憾评判器对候选回复进行重加权，从理论和实证上提高了对抗性对话场景下的安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对潜在有害提问时常常难以区分真实恶意与误解性友好请求，这在多轮对话中尤为棘手：攻击者可能通过多轮逐步暴露意图，而现有安全框架通常把每次回复看作独立的上下文赌博机（contextual bandit），忽视对话轨迹的推断能力。作者提出需要一种能够在每一轮推断用户意图并据此调整生成的机制。

Method: 把对话视作状态转移的MDP，训练两个层次的critic（token级与utterance级）以估计价值与遗憾，候选回复由基策略采样产生，再用action-value critic对这些候选回复打分并重加权采样，数学上等价于对基策略做近似的指数倾斜，从而在有限候选集合下提高期望回报；在多个对抗性对话基准上进行对比测试并做迁移实验。

Result: 提出Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS)，将对话攻击建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在token级和utterance级同时学习基于value和regret的评价器，并用action-value评价器对候选回复重加权。证明该推断时重加权近似基策略的指数倾斜（exponential tilting），对任意有限候选池都能保证期望回报改进。实证上，在CARES-18k、WildJailbreak、Redbench和Harmbench等基准上优于鲁棒基线及前沿模型，并能无微调地迁移到这些前沿模型提升其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将对话安全问题从独立决策扩展为MDP、在推断时使用learned critics对候选回复做指数倾斜式重加权，能在保证理论改进的同时带来实证收益；但方法依赖候选池质量与评价器本身的准确性，部署时需评估效率、误杀率和对抗适应性。

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue. Instead of applying a fixed rule about what is or is not safe, DCGS learns what the user's intent is likely to be based on the full conversational history and generates responses accordingly. Formally, we model adversarial dialogue as a Markov Decision Process and learn value and regret-based critics at both the individual token and utterance (full response) levels, scoring candidate responses via an action-value critic. We prove that this inference-time reweighting approximates exponential tilting of the base policy, guaranteeing improvement in expected return for any finite candidate pool, a property that group-relative objectives do not exhibit. Evaluated on CARES-18k, WildJailbreak, Redbench, and Harmbench, DCGS outperforms strong robust baselines and frontier models on adversarial dialogue tasks. DCGS also transfers to frontier models, improving their robustness without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [22] [Delivery, Not Storage: Cue-Anchored Working Memory as a Harness Property for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20972)
*Swapnanil Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 关键不是存储而是交付：对长运行代理而言，可靠的记忆通道应是harness自动按情境触发并确定性交付的，而非代理显式读写的文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到现有编码代理仅依赖显式文档式记忆，而人类长期运行技能依赖于情境绑定的、无意识检索的二级记忆；主张长期、可靠的代理记忆必须由运行时harness承担，而非代理自主选择。

Method: 提出并实现了一个两层记忆架构：顶层为有意的文档式记忆（agent可写可读），底层为由harness管理的情境触发记忆（incidental encoding/event-based prospective memory），并定义了一个带有一阶触发条件（路径、符号、语义、事件、时间）且由harness确定性评估的cue-anchored记忆模型；通过受控实验（编码任务、重复压缩/恢复探针）验证行为。

Result: 经实验发现：自愿记忆操作接近零（114轮对话中0次）；基于harness的确定性注入在所有预置运行中到达且无误报；39%的会话内重复阅读是为了重新获取在压缩边界之前已付费的内容；仅对话持有的事实在第一次摘要后大多丢失（106/108次），而由harness持有并注入的事实在所有138次压缩-恢复中完整保留。

Conclusion: 要实现长期、可靠的代理记忆，应把情境触发的记忆交由harness作为首要特性来实现（触发词汇化、确定性评估、持久注入及压缩鲁棒性），交付质量比存储容量更关键。

Abstract: Coding agents ship with one kind of memory: documents. Instruction files, plan artifacts, and auto-written memory directories are deliberately authored and deliberately retrieved: the agent must choose to write them and choose to read them back. Human expertise runs on a second tier that never gets written down: situationally-bound operational facts (gotchas, locations, local conventions) encoded as a side effect of the work and retrieved involuntarily when the situation cues them. We argue this second tier is the load-bearing one for long-running agents and must be a harness property, not an agent choice. We contribute: (1) a two-tier design theory grounded in the cognitive literature on memory offloading, incidental encoding, and event-based prospective memory, each mapped to an architectural requirement; (2) a cue-anchored memory model where memories carry first-class trigger conditions over a composable vocabulary (path, symbol, semantic, event, temporal), evaluated deterministically by the harness, a composition no surveyed academic or shipped system provides; (3) a controlled evaluation on a real coding task showing that voluntary memory use is near zero even with a pre-seeded store (0 memory operations in 114 turns), that deterministic injection delivered in every seeded run with zero false alarms, and that 39% of intra-session re-reads re-buy content paid for before a compaction boundary; (4) a repeated-compaction decay probe: ten facts held only in conversation vanish at the first summary and stay absent from 106 of 108 compactions, and the deprived agent greps the harness's own session files to rebuild them, while the same facts injected from a harness-owned store arrive intact through all 138 compact-resumes as the final summary carries none. Delivery, not storage, is the product: the reliable memory channel for agents is the one the agent never has to think about.

</details>


### [23] [Incomplete Prompt Jailbreaks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20473)
*Yeonjea Kim,Bumjin Park,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs can be induced to produce harmful continuations from incomplete harmful prompts (IPJ). Models tend to delay refusal, fine-tuning fails to generalize, and two neurons controlling termination/continuation offer a promising direction for targeted defenses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Open-weight LLMs with content safeguards remain vulnerable to harmful outputs via incomplete prompts; understanding these vulnerabilities and creating robust defenses is critical for safer model deployment.

Method: Empirical characterization of incomplete prompt jailbreaks across attractor types; parameter tuning experiments to assess generalization; neuron analysis to identify termination and continuation neurons and tests of neuron-level interventions.

Result: The paper studies incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) where LLMs produce harmful completions when given incomplete harmful prompts. It formalizes IPJ, characterizes attractor types, finds that models delay refusal until sentence termination, shows that parameter tuning to refuse incomplete prompts fails to generalize, and identifies two functional neurons (termination and continuation) enabling neuron-level interventions for IPJ defenses.

Conclusion: Incomplete prompt jailbreaks exploit LLM tendency to continue incomplete sentences; parameter tuning is insufficient for robust defense, but neuron-level interventions targeting termination and continuation neurons can provide more precise control.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly released as open-weight models with safeguards against harmful requests. Nevertheless, sentence completion remains vulnerable to incomplete harmful prompts. In this work, we formalize this phenomenon as incomplete prompt jailbreaks (IPJ) and provide a systematic empirical characterization of when and how incomplete prompts elicit harmful continuations. We analyze diverse attractor types associated with incomplete sentence continuation and show that LLMs systematically delay refusal until sentence termination. We further demonstrate that training models to refuse incomplete harmful prompts via parameter tuning is insufficient, failing to generalize across both content domains and attractor types. To enable fine-grained control, we identify two functional neurons: termination and continuation neurons. By clarifying their roles in sentence completion, we highlight the potential of neuron-level interventions for more precise and robust IPJ defenses.

</details>


### [24] [HiMe: Real-Time Self-Hosted Personal Agent Platform for Health Insights with Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21019)
*Wei Liu,Siya Qi,Linhai Zhang,Lorainne Tudor Car,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HiMe，一个可本地部署、注重隐私、支持实时健康数据与长期用户建模的个人健康代理平台，通过以数据库为核心并在效能与效率间寻找帕累托最优，支持多款可穿戴设备生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional wearable health analysis is constrained by rigid frameworks and limited personalization; LLM agents offer adaptive, contextual insights but lack an open-source, locally deployable, privacy-preserving platform for real-time personal health data processing.

Method: 架构上以数据库为核心，设计实时数据管道并结合长期用户画像，采用对效能与效率联合优化的设计策略以降低计算/能耗成本；通过与多种可穿戴设备生态兼容实现数据输入。抽象地可能包含本地LLM代理、流处理模块、长期特征存储与策略模块，但摘要未给出具体算法、模型或评估细节。

Result: HiMe: a locally deployable, privacy-first agent platform compatible with diverse wearable ecosystems. It enforces three design principles—database as first-class, joint optimisation of effectiveness and efficiency for Pareto-optimality, and real-time data processing with long-term user modelling—enabling continuous, personalized monitoring.

Conclusion: HiMe 将个人健康代理从云依赖、单一分析框架推进到可本地部署、实时且个性化的解决方案，若能补充具体实现、评估与隐私保障细节，将对个人健康监测与干预产生实际影响。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to wearable health signal analysis, such as smartwatches, are constrained by rigid analytical frameworks and limited personalisation. The emergence of LLM agents creates a new opportunity for Personal Health Agentic Analysis, where health insights can be generated adaptively and in context. However, currently there is no open-source locally deployable platform capable of processing personal health data in real time while preserving privacy. We present HiMe, a locally deployable, privacy-first agent platform that is fully compatible with real-time health data ecosystems across a wide range of wearable devices. HiMe is guided by three design principles. The database is treated as a first-class component. Effectiveness and efficiency are jointly optimised to achieve a low-cost Pareto-optimal balance. Data are processed in real time while the user is modelled over the long term. Together, these principles make it practical for individuals to harness Personal Health Agents for continuous, personalised health monitoring for better wellbeing.

</details>


### [25] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSimpl 提出用“简化型验证”（simplification-based verification）把求解器用于生成关于候选模型的简化诊断查询，帮助 LLM 在局部固定上下文下就模型是否实现意图进行可处理的推理，从而提高从自然语言到优化模型的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 直接从文本生成可执行求解模型会出现语义错误（尽管可执行），难以保证实现了用户意图；需要一种可验证且可修正的机制来提高可靠性。

Method: 对 LLM 生成的候选优化模型，使用求解器或求解器驱动的程序构造针对性简化问题（沿约束或决策变量维度），把全局问题固定为局部情形，令 LLM 在这些更小、可判定的情境中检查并修正模型。通过多种简化策略产生诊断查询并整合其反馈来判别或改进候选模型。

Result: 在若干优化建模基准上，VeriSimpl 相较既有方法显示出一致的准确率提升，并提供了一个置信度高的自我验证信号，表明该框架在提高自然语言到优化模型映射的健壮性方面有效。

Conclusion: 通过将求解器生成的简化诊断查询与 LLM 推理结合，VeriSimpl 在多项基准上显著提高了建模准确性并能给出高精度的自我验证信号，证明简化型验证是一条有效路线。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [26] [Euclid-MCP: A Model Context Protocol Server for Deterministic Logical Reasoning via Prolog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21412)
*Bartolomeo Bogliolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Euclid‑MCP 用可读的 Horn 子句中间表示和 SWI‑Prolog 后端，为 LLM 客户提供可审计、确定性的逻辑推理能力，能显著降低大规模知识库场景下的幻觉与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型在多步逻辑推理中不可靠、尤其在安全或合规场景下容易幻觉的问题；提供一个标准化、可复用的符号推理后端，便于 RAG 或代理系统委托确定性推理并获取完整证明踪迹。

Method: 将神经模型与外部符号推理引擎（SWI‑Prolog）耦合，通过开放的 MCP 服务提供确定性逻辑推理；引入中间表示 Euclid‑IR（Horn 子句为主、可读、易于 LLM 生成）并暴露一个支持 translate→run→inspect→repair 循环的精简工具接口。

Result: 实现了开源 Euclid‑MCP 服务器和 Euclid‑IR，证明在 IT 安全与合规用例中，LLM 单独在小知识库上可行，但在较大问题上会系统性幻觉；Euclid‑MCP 提供精确答案、较低延迟和更简洁输出；主张语义 RAG 不适合作为规则强制手段，Euclid‑MCP 可作为共享的稳定推理基座。

Conclusion: 方法有明确工程与可复用价值，关键创新在于易于生成的 Euclid‑IR 与 translate‑run‑inspect‑repair 工作流；需要更完整的基准、复杂性分析、非 Horn 支持及安全性/可伸缩性评估来巩固结论与推广价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at natural language understanding and generation but remain unreliable for multi-step logical reasoning, especially in safety-critical or compliance-sensitive domains. Recent neuro-symbolic approaches address this gap by coupling neural models with external symbolic engines, yet most integrations are bespoke and lack a standardized interface for tool-augmented agents. This paper presents Euclid-MCP, an open-source MCP server that provides deterministic logical reasoning via SWI-Prolog. Euclid-MCP introduces Euclid-IR, an engine-agnostic intermediate representation for Horn-clause logic that is human-readable, easy for LLMs to generate, and straightforward to compile into Prolog or alternative backends. The server exposes a compact tool interface that supports a translate-run-inspect-repair loop, enabling LLM clients to delegate inference while retaining full access to proof traces and derivation logs. We evaluate Euclid-MCP on a realistic IT security and compliance use case. Results show that while LLMs alone are sufficient on small knowledge bases, they hallucinate systematically on larger problems, whereas Euclid-MCP delivers exact answers with lower latency and more compact outputs. We argue that semantic RAG is fundamentally unsuited for rule enforcement, and that Euclid-MCP can serve as a stable, shared reasoning substrate for both RAG-based assistants and agentic systems.

</details>


### [27] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler 用 tile-aware Triton 内核把完整采样流程融合到单个可 CUDA Graph 捕获的批处理内核里，提出分层两阶段 top-k，并在现实异构采样场景下实现了显著加速（top-k 约 10x，整体最多 16x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实现只加速采样流水线的子集、需要多次内核启动或假定批次内采样行为同质，导致对动态服务场景支持差以及无法高效利用 CUDA Graph；因此需要一种统一、高效且支持异构采样行为的实现。

Method: 提出了一组面向 tile 的 Triton 内核，采用分层两阶段 top-k 算法，并在一个批量内核中实现了 logit 处理、token 选择与推测验证等所有采样步骤，从而减少内核启动和内存移动，利用低熵输出特性加速大词表选择。

Result: 在各类异构的推测解码工作负载上，相较于最先进基线，SonicSampler 在 top-k 算法上可达 10x 加速，整套采样流程上最高可达 16x 加速，并保持灵活的批量执行与 CUDA Graph 兼容性。

Conclusion: SonicSampler 将完整的采样流水线垂直融合到单个、面向工作负载的 Triton 内核中，支持动态的每请求采样行为并兼容 CUDA Graph，显著提升了 top-k 和整套采样流程的性能。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [28] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Tested LLMs reliably detect single-sensor threshold breaches but systematically miss combinatorial/sub-threshold multisensor risk patterns; practitioners should not rely on these models alone for safety monitoring without additional rules or fusion logic.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evaluate whether current large language models can reliably assess multisensor physical-hazard inputs and issue precautionary warnings when several sensors are concurrently elevated but remain individually below their safety thresholds.

Method: Empirical benchmark: 60 designed scenarios in three categories (multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, pattern disambiguation), 5 LLMs (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B), 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, evaluation metrics comparing model outputs on warning issuance and response proportionality; comparison of output formatting (tabular vs prose) and statistical testing for format effects (e.g., ChatGPT-4o prose advantage p=0.001).

Result: Across 1,800 deterministic calls (temperature=0.0) on 60 scenarios, five LLMs failed to emit precautionary warnings in multi-sensor sub-threshold elevation cases (Category A), scoring near zero (Q2: 0.000–0.208; Q3: 0.000–0.592), while achieving near-perfect detection of single-sensor threshold violations (Category B Q1: 0.975–1.000). Structured tables offered no consistent improvement over prose; ChatGPT-4o performed better with prose (p=0.001).

Conclusion: The tested models show a critical blind spot for multisensor joint risk assessment. This necessitates either retraining/fine-tuning with explicit multisensor examples, hybrid systems that combine rule-based fusion with LLM reasoning, or strict operational constraints and human oversight before deployment in safety-critical monitoring.

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [29] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Wasserstein距离的半监督TAG蒸馏框架，采用双路径协同编码、WSD图压缩与聚类关键词驱动的文本合成，实现了在GNN/LLM任务上的高效、可读压缩与优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文本属性图（TAG）结合图拓扑与丰富文本语义，但现有表示学习方法在与大型语言模型（LLM）联合使用时存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈。基于数据蒸馏的思路能够从数据侧缓解问题，但现有方法未能充分建模图-文本模态间复杂相互作用、难以应对半监督下的标签稀缺，并且无法产出下游LLM任务所需的人可读文本属性。

Method: 关键方法包括：双路径编码器设计（graph-aware encoder捕捉拓扑依赖；graph-free encoder侧重文本语义），协同自训练以生成高质量伪标签并融合特征；基于Wasserstein距离的图Sketch算法，具有理论保证的结构与分布近似；以及基于类簇关键词抽取的LLM文本合成模块，用以生成压缩节点的摘要文本，整体为半监督训练流程提供数据与模型层面的协同优化。

Result: 提出了一种名为\algo{}的统一半监督框架，以Wasserstein距离（WSD）为指导。框架包含：图-文本协同编码模块（双路径编码器：graph-aware与graph-free）及协同自训练以获取可靠伪标签并融合互补特征；一个有理论支撑的WSD驱动图压缩（graph sketching）算法；以及基于聚类关键词抽取的低成本LLM文本合成模块，用于为压缩后的节点生成连贯可读摘要。大量基准实验表明，\algo{}在GNN与LLM下游任务上达成了最优的性能-压缩权衡。

Conclusion: \algo{}通过结合协同自训练与WSD理论化的图压缩，加上面向LLM的文本合成，既能在半监督场景下缓解标签稀缺，也能产出供LLM使用的人可读属性，从而在性能与效率上取得良好折中，适用于大规模TAG学习与分析。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Detection of LLM deception is highly sensitive to representation choice and training lie typology; no single probe or layer universally outperforms others, and augmenting training with diverse deception types changes detectability patterns.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deceptive outputs from large language models are hard to detect, and detection probes trained on one lie type generalize poorly to other lie types. The paper aims to systematically study which factors (representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse features, lie typology in training data) influence probe performance and cross-domain generalization.

Method: Systematic empirical study: augment standard benchmark training sets with a supplementary, diverse deception dataset (fabrication, omission, exaggeration), evaluate seven probe architectures varying in expressivity, test features from different model depths and both dense and sparse autoencoder representations, and measure cross-type generalization and in-domain performance.

Result: Across experiments with seven probe types and augmented training data containing fabrication, omission, and exaggeration, the authors find (1) optimal representation depth varies by dataset, (2) more expressive probes give only selective improvements over linear probes, (3) sparse autoencoder-derived features perform comparably to dense hidden states, and (4) the choice of training data and lie typology substantially affects detectability, indicating representation-dependent detection.

Conclusion: Deception detection lacks a one-size-fits-all solution: performance depends strongly on which hidden-layer representations and training lie types are used. Future progress requires careful selection of representations and more diverse, typology-aware training data.

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [31] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于受限局部重连与物理/空间约束的配电拓扑识别框架，并用伪证驱动的可靠性度量辅助核验，在真实大型AMI数据上实现高准确率与良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际配电网的连通性记录不完备且元数据不一致，单纯依赖电气相似性或空间记录在密集馈线和不一致元数据条件下会失效，需要一种能融合多源不确定证据并保证物理与空间可行性的可扩展方法。

Method: 不重建全局连通性，而是检测与基础拓扑不一致的分配，在受限邻域内进行局部重连以保证可扩展性，并通过迭代施加物理可行性（如电压/潮流约束）和空间约束来获得最终拓扑。引入伪证（falsification）驱动的度量，对每条推断连接相对于其它可行替代方案的支持度进行量化。

Result: 在与一家大型美式公用事业合作的三个馈线（>8,000个AMI用户）上验证，达到超过95%的拓扑重构准确率，同时相较于全局推断方法显著减少计算开销；证明了仅相关性方法在城市密集馈线上会产生歧义，而结合测量、空间与运行约束可实现稳健可扩展的拓扑恢复。

Conclusion: 本文将配电网拓扑识别表述为在空间可行性和物理运行约束下，对公用事业提供的基础拓扑进行基于异构证据的约束推断与局部重连，从而产生运维一致的拓扑估计，并提出基于“反证驱动”的连接可靠性度量以指导人工核查。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [32] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [33] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了以真实浏览轨迹为历史的PersonaTrail基准和将历史分解为事实/偏好记忆的PACMem框架，通过相关条目检索改善个性化网页代理的偏好推断与记忆回忆能力，并在基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中用户常给出欠规格指令，网页代理需从用户的原始浏览历史中推断缺失上下文；现有基准不能充分模拟这种个性化记忆检索与偏好推断需求。

Method: 构建由真实浏览轨迹构成的PersonaTrail基准；设计Preference-Aware Contextual Memory（PACMem），将浏览历史结构化为事实记忆（会话摘要）与偏好记忆（重复行为模式），并在推理时检索最相关条目以指导个性化导航；与若干记忆基线进行离线实验评估。

Result: 在PersonaTrail上，PACMem在两个评估任务上均显著优于现有基于记忆的基线，展示了结构化记忆（事实+偏好）在个性化网页导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: 作者提出了PersonaTrail基准与PACMem框架，通过将原始浏览轨迹分解为事实记忆与偏好记忆并在推理时检索相关条目，提升了个性化网页代理在从历史中推断偏好和回忆信息任务上的性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [34] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are fundamentally limited by representation collapse, a bottleneck that severely degrades long-context performance. We identify that existing approaches risk drifting into one of two pathological extremes: homogenization collapse (e.g., attention sinks causing rank deficiency) and isolation collapse (e.g., local attention causing context disconnection). Through spectral analysis of attention dynamics, we derive an intrinsic trade-off between mixing efficiency (spectral gap) and information capacity (effective rank) that standard mechanisms struggle to balance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose the Topologically Regularized Side-Path (TRSP), a non-invasive architectural intervention that achieves spectral balance. TRSP employs a parameter-free Triangular Box mechanism, scaled by a lightweight, length-aware gate, to regularize the token interaction topology. By integrating proximal coupling to preserve effective rank and distal propagation to support non-degenerate mixing, TRSP promotes a geometrically healthier transition operator without altering core attention. Experiments show significant improvements across general capabilities and long-context benchmarks. Notably, on NoLiMa at $8\times$ the training length, TRSP retains $83\%$ accuracy and surpasses the Differential Transformer and Gated Attention by approximately 30 and 50 percentage points, respectively. Code available at: https://github.com/Eziotao-tyd/TRSP.

</details>


### [36] [Expectation Alignment of Language Models for Real-World User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20485)
*Miaomiao Li,Yang Wang,Bin Liang,Shudong Liu,Zhiwei Zhang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: First benchmark built from real user expectations; shows misalignment of current LLMs; LENS models latent expectations to improve alignment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing evaluations don't reflect diverse, subtle real user expectations, causing apparent competence but real-world misalignment; need benchmark and methods to model and satisfy expectations.

Method: Systematic procedure to extract semantically rich expectations from real interactions, create ExpectBench, analyze model behavior, and design LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework that internalizes expectations to guide responses.

Result: Paper constructs ExpectBench from real user expectations and shows existing LLMs fail to satisfy/anticipate expectations; proposes LENS, a latent expectation-aware generation framework that improves satisfaction.

Conclusion: Explicitly modeling latent user expectations improves LLM response alignment and user satisfaction; existing heuristics/expert rubrics fail to capture diverse real-world expectations.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on standard benchmarks, yet it remains largely unexplored whether they truly meet user expectations. Existing evaluation approaches, relying on model heuristics, expert rubrics, or user simulation, fail to capture the diversity and subtlety of real human expectations, causing models to appear competent while misaligning with what users actually seek. We present the first systematic study of user expectations in real-world LLM interactions, proposing a principled procedure to extract semantically rich expectations and introducing ExpectBench, a benchmark grounded in real user expectations. Analyses reveal that current LLMs struggle to satisfy and anticipate what users hope to obtain, highlighting a fundamental source of misalignment. Building on these observations, we propose LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework. LENS enables models to internalize user expectations and generate better-aligned responses, consistently improving expectation satisfaction and underscoring the importance of explicitly modeling user expectations for realistic human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [37] [OPTScientist: Multi-Agent Discovery of Typed Optimizer Programs for Transformer Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20486)
*Zhongzheng Li,Tiancan Feng,Wenhao Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPTScientist，一个理论引导的多智能体在类型化DSL上发现优化器的框架，通过模块化表达更新、四角色协作、进化搜索及DSL小扩展，发现了提升Transformer预训练的RS-MR优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化器发现方法要么搜索无约束代码空间导致非法或难以解释的程序，要么受限于窄幅参数化族限制创新，需要一种兼顾稳健性、可解释性与发现新颖策略的自动化流程。

Method: 定义方向、缩放、预处理、正则、状态、分组六类模块的类型化DSL；四角色（Theorist, Designer, Engineer, Reviewer）在循环中提出假设、合成DSL候选、编译并评估优化器、批判结果；采用进化搜索生成程序，并在重复失败时触发小范围DSL扩展；利用编译器验证与闭环实验筛选与迭代优化器。

Result: 在作者的评估协议下，发现了RS-MR（一种简化状态的矩阵优化器），在Transformer预训练上超过强基线；同时展示了理论引导、类型化DSL与闭环实验相结合的可行性。

Conclusion: OPTScientist能在受约束的程序空间中自动发现实用、可验证的优化器，平衡可解释性与新颖性，展示了自动化优化器科学的可行路线。

Abstract: Designing optimizers for modern deep learning remains a challenging scientific problem, requiring the joint consideration of optimization geometry, state dynamics, numerical stability, implementation constraints, and empirical generalization. Existing automated optimizer discovery methods typically search either over unconstrained code spaces or within narrowly parameterized optimizer families. The former is flexible but often produces invalid or uninterpretable programs, while the latter is stable but limits novelty. We introduce OPTScientist, a theory-guided multi-agent framework for optimizer discovery in a typed domain-specific language (DSL). OPTScientist formulates optimizer design as a constrained scientific search process, where candidate updates are expressed through direction, scaling, preconditioning, regularization, state, and grouping modules. Four role agents, Theorist, Designer, Engineer, and Reviewer, collaborate within a single orchestration loop to propose hypotheses, synthesize DSL candidates, compile and evaluate optimizers, and critique results. To overcome the limitations of a fixed search space, OPTScientist combines evolutionary search over optimizer programs with a second-stage mechanism that proposes small DSL extensions when repeated failures reveal representational bottlenecks. Using this framework, we discover RS-MR, a reduced-state matrix optimizer that improves transformer pretraining over strong baselines under our native evaluation protocol. Our results suggest a path toward automated optimizer science grounded in theory, typed programs, compiler validation, and closed-loop experimentation.

</details>


### [38] [Directional Hallucinations: Ideological Drift in News-Grounded LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20487)
*Chendi Wang,Liam Cunningham,Tom Yishay,Jieying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在基于文档的政治QA中，幻觉是意识形态偏移的诊断信号，表现为左倾内容的过度生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM在高风险政治信息环境下的事实/意识形态偏差，指导审计和安全措施。

Method: 对21727篇带标签的美政新闻生成问题，使用多模型回答，基于参考进行句级幻觉检测，微调立场分类器判定幻觉倾向，并分析输出logit与熵。

Result: 发现LLM在政治新闻问答中产生幻觉，幻觉内容偏向左翼，幻觉发生率与来源意识形态关系微弱，且与高不确定性相关。

Conclusion: LLM幻觉内容表现出稳健的左倾偏移，需在选举相关部署中采取审计与保护措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about political information, including in election-adjacent information settings where factual errors and ideological distortions are high-stakes. We present a reproducible measurement framework that treats hallucinations, unsupported statements in document-grounded QA, as diagnostic signals of ideological drift. Using 21,727 expert-labeled U.S. political news articles from QBias spanning left, center, and right sources, we (i) generate an article-specific question, (ii) elicit document-grounded answers from three open-weight LLMs and one proprietary model, (iii) detect sentence-level hallucinations via reference-based comparison, (iv) classify the ideological valence of hallucinated sentences with a fine-tuned stance classifier, and (v) probe output logits to relate token-level uncertainty to hallucination and drift. Hallucination rates vary substantially across models and concentrate in contentious topics, while source-ideology differences in hallucination frequency are modest. In contrast, hallucination content exhibits robust leftward drift: a majority of hallucinated sentences are classified as left-leaning, including among hallucinations generated from right-leaning sources. Logit-level analysis shows hallucinations arise in high-entropy generation contexts, and in some models uncertainty also predicts leftward drift, consistent with an "uncertainty to guessing" mechanism. We discuss implications for auditing AI-mediated political information and for designing safeguards in election-relevant deployments.

</details>


### [39] [Autonomous Topology Mutation: Safe Runtime Restructuring for Multi-Agent LLM Systems with Capability, State, and Shadow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20488)
*Bronislav Sidik,Chaya Levi,Nizzan Kimhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATM：一种基于遥测的运行时拓扑变异机制，通过六信号瓶颈指数检测过载，因子化过载agent并用三条不变式保障安全与隐私；实验显示大幅提升成功率、减少隐私暴露且运行开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在启动时固定拓扑，但运行时负载变化会导致个别agent过载，现有框架缺乏自动重构能力；因此需要一种可在运行时安全地检测过载并重构团队拓扑以恢复性能与保障隐私。

Method: 1) 定义六信号瓶颈指数（队列深度、上下文抖动、工具错误率、角色熵、重试循环率、跨代理等待时间）；2) 通过warmup校准的阈值检测连续违规ticks触发因子化；3) 将父agent替换为协调器并将状态按隐私级别路由到允许子集或显式丢弃；4) 在将任何候选拓扑切换到真实流量前进行影子验证窗口；5) 对比消融实验和三个工作负载，在DeepSeek-V3基准上评估成功率、隐私泄露事件与延迟。

Result: ATM provides a runtime mechanism to detect overloaded agents in a multi-agent LLM system and mutate team topology by factorising overloaded agents into specialised sub-agents, preserving external identity via a coordinator role, with three safety invariants (capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, shadow-before-live validation). It uses a six-signal Bottleneck Index for overload detection, privacy-aware state routing, and shadow validation before live traffic. Experiments (720 runs on DeepSeek-V3, deterministic tool stubs) show large gains in code-task success (3.3% -> 61.7%) and reduced high-privacy memory exposures (2.0 -> 0.0 per task) with low runtime latency overhead (<500 µs p99). The implementation and benchmarks are open-sourced.

Conclusion: ATM是一种切实可行的运行时多智能体拓扑重构方法，能在保持外部一致性的同时，自动分解过载节点并用隐私感知的状态路由和影子验证把控风险；在合成基准上显著提升任务成功率并降低敏感信息暴露，且延迟开销微小。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM frameworks typically fix their team topology at boot time. When an individual agent becomes overloaded at runtime, for example by mixing too many action categories, accumulating tool errors, or queueing behind too many calls, the system has no mechanism to restructure itself. We introduce Autonomous Topology Mutation (ATM), a runtime team-mutation mechanism for multi-agent LLM frameworks. ATM combines telemetry-driven overload detection with three safety invariants that gate each structural change: capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, and shadow-before-live validation.
  ATM monitors a six-signal Bottleneck Index that includes queue depth, context thrash, tool-error rate, role entropy, retry-loop rate, and cross-agent wait time. When a warmup-calibrated threshold is breached for multiple consecutive ticks, ATM factorises the overloaded agent into specialised sub-agents and hot-swaps the parent into a coordinator role while preserving its external identity. State transfer is controlled by privacy-level-aware routing: each memory atom is routed only to a permitted child set, or explicitly dropped with a logged reason. No candidate topology receives live traffic until it has passed a shadow validation window.
  On 720 DeepSeek-V3-driven task runs with deterministic tool stubs across four ablation conditions and three workloads, the ATM factoriser split lifts code-task success from 3.3% to 61.7%. The full rail-and-distillation system reduces detected high-privacy memory exposure under a regex classifier from 2.0 to 0.0 events per task while preserving task quality. The runtime rails carrying ATM's invariants add less than 500 microseconds of p99 latency on the agent hot path. A small live-tool probe with real Python execution is included as an external-validity check. The implementation, benchmark harness, and traces are open-sourced.

</details>
